# ⚔️ Weapon: 2010 Blastoff Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [2010 Blastoff Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/2010_Blastoff_Trap)

The 2010 Blastoff Trap is a Limited Edition Tactical type weapon.

| 2010 Blastoff Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Tactical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,600 | Title Required: | Journeyman |
| Power Bonus: | 15% | Cost: | 512,800 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 25 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Very Stale | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| It's time to welcome in the new year! Lying in wait behind this colorful pile of fireworks is a diligent little droid, waiting to make the first catch of 2010. When a mouse takes the bait, the droid pushes the plunger, and in a blinding flash of light and color the mouse sails up into the stratosphere, tied to a colossal rocket by the rope around his foot. The mouse can use the banner as a parachute, if he's smart, but that all depends on whether or not the banner catches fire during launch. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The 2010 Blastoff Trap is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It was available to purchase from Ronza's Traveling Shoppe during The Great Winter Hunt '09. It cost 512,800 gold and could be refunded for 92,304 gold at the same location.

## Related Weapons

- 2012 Big Boom Trap
- A complete list of Tactical weapons can be found here.

# ⚔️ Weapon: 2012 Big Boom Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [2012 Big Boom Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/2012_Big_Boom_Trap)

The 2012 Big Boom Trap is a Limited Edition Tactical type weapon.

| 2012 Big Boom Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Tactical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,500 | Title Required: | Journeyman |

| Luck: | 25 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Extremely Stale | Smashable: | No |

| A fine and attractive looking trap for any mouse who likes to have a good time, it is so attractive and dazzling in fact that when the volley of non-lethal BOOM! brand light-works whiz past them, the mouse is rendered immobile for minutes after from sheer awe at the beauty... Except of course when the hunter loads the fireworks volley with BOOM! brand high-incendiary fire-work rounds; in that case you definitely won't be finding a mouse at all. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The 2012 Big Boom Trap is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It was available to purchase from the Festive Comet Trapsmith for 600,000 gold and 100 Firecracker Charms, and required that the hunter possess at least 600 Festive Toy Receipts. It could be refunded at the same location for 108,000 gold.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Admiral's Galleon Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Admiral's Galleon Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Admiral's_Galleon_Trap)

The Admiral's Galleon Trap is a Limited Edition Shadow type weapon.

| Admiral's Galleon Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Shadow |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,500 | Title Required: | Lord |
| Power Bonus: | 25% | Cost: | 0 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 15 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | No effect | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| A massive haunted galleon once commanded by Admiral Arrrgh himself. It strikes fear in the souls of all nether-nautical ghostship captains and mice alike. Sitting idly by, hovering between this world and the next, it waits, invisibly, for an unsuspecting mouse to step aboard the base. Once awoken, it materializes and begins to lift into the sky with its new member of the crew.<br>Of course they always have the option to jump...<br>The Admiral's Galleon Trap has the spooktacular ability to randomly raid mice for up to 5,000 bonus Cursed Gold! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Admiral's Galleon Trap is an Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It was not sold by any Trapsmith. It could only be obtained by defeating 1 Ghostship during the 2018 Halloween event.

## Special Effects

- The Admiral's Galleon Trap has the ability to randomly raid mice for up to 5,000 bonus Cursed Gold.
- Spooky Bonus: The Admiral's Galleon Trap acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Spooky Charm variant.

## Skins

- Goody Galleon Trap Skin

# ⚔️ Weapon: Ambush Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Ambush Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Ambush_Trap)

: "Ambush" redirects here. For other uses, see Ambush (disambiguation).

The Ambush Trap is a Tactical type weapon.

| Ambush Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Tactical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 3,000 | Title Required: | Grandmaster |
| Power Bonus: | 5% | Cost: | 63,410 Gold1 Master's Seal1 Ambush Trap Blueprints |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 12 | Crafted: | Yes |
| Cheese Effect: | Fresh | Smashable: | Yes |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| This peaceful setting for tea and a light snack appears to be the perfect spot for war-hardened mice to pause for some peaceful rest and meditation. Yet behind a thin paper wall lies a robot ambush of epic proportions! The loyal droids follow the will of their controlling hunter to either capture and exile mice or use their high speed reflexes for a heavy battle that is sure to erupt into various explosions and montages. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Ambush Trap can be purchased from Trapsmith in Dojo, Meditation Room, and Pinnacle Chamber for:

- 63,410 Gold
- 1 Master's Seal
- 1 Ambush Trap Blueprints

It cannot be refunded.

The Ambush Trap (if unintentionally smashed) can be reassembled from Ambush Trap Parts.

### Formula

| Reconstruction Formula: Rebuild Ambush Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Recipe can be unlocked by: | Catch any mouse with Ambush Trap armed |  |  |
| Item | Qty | Cost (Each) | Source |
| Ambush Trap Parts | 1 | 63,410 | Use the Hunter's Hammer to smash the Ambush Trap |
| Total | 63,410 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

## Skins

- Costumed Ambush Skin

## Hunter's Hammer

- Smashing this weapon with the Hunter's Hammer yields Ambush Trap Parts.

## Related Items

- Ninja Ambush Trap - once requires Ambush Trap Parts to craft

# ⚔️ Weapon: Ancient Box Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Ancient Box Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Ancient_Box_Trap)

The Ancient Box Trap is a Forgotten type weapon.

| Ancient Box Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Forgotten |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 4,300 | Title Required: | Hero |
| Power Bonus: | 10% | Cost: | 158,175 Gold1 Ancient Ripped Blueprint Piece1 Ancient Frayed Blueprint Piece1 Ancient Mangled Blueprint Piece1 Ancient Relic Staff1 Timeless Mystic Gem1 Hinge of Eternity1 Ethereal Rope1 Engraved Solid Stone Slab1 Encrusted Metal of Time |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 4 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| The ancient ghost mice which roamed Gnawnia before even the castle existed require an old-fashioned trap strategy, and the Ancient Box Trap is about as old-fashioned as they come. A heavy stone box teeters precariously upon an Ancient Relic Staff, waiting for a mouse to pull the Ethereal Rope and cause the box to slam shut and magically fuse with the base. This may seem like a deceptively simple trap set-up, but ancient mice enjoy the classics. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Ancient Box Trap can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Cape Clawed, Jungle of Dread, and Balack's Cove for:

- 158,175 Gold
- 1 Ancient Ripped Blueprint Piece
- 1 Ancient Frayed Blueprint Piece
- 1 Ancient Mangled Blueprint Piece
- 1 Ancient Relic Staff
- 1 Timeless Mystic Gem
- 1 Hinge of Eternity
- 1 Ethereal Rope
- 1 Engraved Solid Stone Slab
- 1 Encrusted Metal of Time

It cannot be refunded.

## Skins

# ⚔️ Weapon: Arcane

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Arcane](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Arcane)

## Overview

Arcane is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Arcane power type is symbolized by an image of a hand holding an orb. Image

Hunters can change their power type to Arcane by arming either:

- A Weapon with Arcane power type
- The Golem Guardian Arcane Skin Module

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Arcane traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

| Indigenous Mice<br>Dock Dwellers<br>Mountain Mice<br>Forest Guild<br>Lab Experiments<br>The Shadow Clan | Digby Dirt Dwellers<br>The Forgotten Mice<br>Balack's Banished<br>Gauntlet Gladiators<br>Meteorite Miners<br>The Marching Flame | Lost City Mice<br>Queso Canyoneers<br>Citizens of Zokor<br>Moussu Picchu Inhabitants<br>Floating Islanders<br>Rift Walkers | Event Mice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Arcane Capturing Rod Of Never Yielding Mystery](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Arcane_Capturing_Rod_Of_Never_Yielding_Mystery)

The Arcane Capturing Rod Of Never Yielding Mystery (or A.C.R.O.N.Y.M.) is an Arcane type weapon.

| Arcane Capturing Rod Of Never Yielding Mystery |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Arcane |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |

| This highly unstable trap setup is held in a constant state of metamorphosis by the unpredictable power of the Onyx stone. This trap almost appears alive, growing metal roots into whatever base to which it is attached.This trap's methods of capture are infinitely random. It can use its jagged metallic roots to impale mice, expel a thick and dark vapor that turns mice into stone, or bend space and time to erase the mouse from existence.Scientists of Digby warn hunters that the power of the Onyx stone is incredibly dangerous and fear that using this trap could lead to the end of the world. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Arcane Capturing Rod Of Never Yielding Mystery can be purchased from Trapsmith in Catacombs, Forbidden Grove, Acolyte Realm, Balack's Cove, and Fort Rox for:

- 632,700 Gold
- 12 Scrap Metal
- 1 Onyx Stone
- 3 Magic Essence
- 1 Obelisk Parts

It cannot be refunded.

The Arcane Capturing Rod Of Never Yielding Mystery (if unintentionally smashed) can be reassembled from A.C.R.O.N.Y.M. Parts.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Boiling Cauldron Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Boiling Cauldron Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Boiling_Cauldron_Trap)

Boiling Cauldron Trap is a Halloween Weapon with potion-generating effects.

| Boiling Cauldron Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Arcane |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 4,500 | Title Required: | Recruit |
| Power Bonus: | 15% | Cost: | See below |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 20 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Stale | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Baba Gaga created this ornate cauldron by harnessing the arcane energies that grow naturally in the enchanted vines of the Gloomy Greenwood. She built it for the purpose of brewing magical potions powerful enough to defeat all the hunters of Gnawnia so it was poetic justice that those same hunters learned how to turn her creation against her!<br>This cauldron has been repurposed into a devastating mouse trap that lures in mice who seek to absorb some of its arcane energy but instead find themselves engulfed in a very mysterious and very unpleasant boiling liquid. Most of the time this liquid causes the mouse to vanish into thin air but every once in a while, it actually transforms the unfortunate creature into a useful potion!<br>This trap has a rare chance to produce a random potion after catching a mouse.<br>When armed with the Alchemist's Cookbook Base, the base is guaranteed to duplicate any potion that this trap produces. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

This weapon is available during the annual Halloween event:

- 2021: from the Reward Track after brewing 3 batches of Evil Extract
- 2022: purchased in the Gloomy Greenwood Trapsmith for 2,260,600 gold
- 2023: purchased in the Gloomy Greenwood Trapsmith for 2,260,600 gold

## Special Effects

- Has a chance to produce a random Non-event potion or Specialty potion upon catching a mouse
This effect is doubled when paired with Alchemist's Cookbook Base.
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:

- Spooky Bonus: The Boiling Cauldron Trap acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Spooky Charm variant.

| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |

| From deep within the bowls of Dr. F. Romage's laboratory comes this menacing machine. Personally responsible for accruing his supply of zombie brains for his dastardly and devious experiments, this machine can seamlessly extract a zombie mouse brain at the rate of approximately one brain every fifteen minutes.Scarily straight forward and easy to use, this device can remove zombie brains automatically once it is armed. ## Obtained Via

The Brain Extractor is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It could only be bought from the Trapsmith in the Haunted Terrortories for 237,000 gold, 1 Brain Extractor Piece and 10 Halloween Candies during the Halloween 2013 event. It could not be refunded.

The Brain Extractor (if unintentionally smashed) can be reassembled from Brain Extractor Parts.

## Related Items

### Weapons

- Maniacal Brain Extractor - Requires the Brain Extractor Parts. An upgraded version of the Brain Extractor that has the same special effects and more power and luck.

### Bases

- Skello-ton Base - Increases the special effects of the Brain Extractor and Maniacal Brain Extractor weapons.

### Charms

- Brain Charm - Require Brain Bits to craft and increase the encounter rate of the various zombie mice.
- Super Brain Charm - Require Brain Bits to craft and increase the encounter rate of the various zombie mice.

## Hunter's Hammer

Smashing this weapon with the Hunter's Hammer yields Brain Extractor Parts, used in crafting the Maniacal Brain Extractor.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Chesla's Revenge Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Chesla's Revenge Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Chesla's_Revenge_Trap)

The Chesla's Revenge Trap is a Limited Edition Tactical weapon.

| Chesla's Revenge Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Tactical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 5,000 | Title Required: | Lord/Lady |
| Power Bonus: | 2% | Cost: | 0 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 16 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Extremely Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Hunting with the Chesla's Revenge Trap in the Seasonal Garden has the chance to overload it and increase the amount of amplification gained per catch. Just... keep your distance...<br>Anna Chesla was a world-renowned inventor and chess master, best known for her patented "Advanced Chess" method. Her final invention, Chesla's Revenge, was named by her followers after her mysterious disappearance who saw it as one last attempt to best her bitter arch-rival, Zugzwang.<br>Ancient rival of Zugzwang, Chesla was never able to defeat the chess master and would frustratingly return time and time again to the Seasonal Garden, biding her time until she was charged up for another attempt. With near perfect mastery of the technological advances of the time, she refused to admit her lack of mastery for chess.<br>With the focus on charging her Amplifier to enter the tower, she couldn't spare much time for focus on improving her chess tactics. Out of her frustration, she had an epiphany! Using her great technological prowess, she quickly built an extremely powerful coil to gather and focus the magic energies of the Seasonal Garden, greatly accelerating her amplification rate!<br>Finally being able to focus her energies elsewhere, she had turned up the power too high in anticipation and overloaded the coils. When the bright glow from the coil finally dimmed, she was never seen again.<br>The power from the coil was not enough to destroy her, and many conflicting theories about her disappearance surfaced over the years, but no one truly knows what became of Anna...<br>Scrawled upon the underside of the tower, one can make out the word "Checkmate". |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Chesla's Revenge Trap is a limited edition weapon not sold by any Trapsmith, and is therefore non-refundable. It can only be acquired through the opening of a Season 2 Treasure Chest:

## Location-Specific Effects

- Seasonal Garden
Has a chance to double Amplifier gained from catching a mouse

| Circlet of Pursuing Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

It can predict the movements of its quarry with remarkable accuracy which allows it to launch its golden circlet with impeccable timing and absolute precision. ### Formula

Blueprint skins are applied to the weapon by crafting it with the smashed trap parts. Unlike removable trap skins, the process is irreversible and the trap cannot be used to complete a few specific tasks in Library Assignments. Weapons with blueprint skins appear on the Hunter's Profile as a different item from their original counterparts and cannot be smashed with the Hunter's Hammer.

| This ornate and ominous three-sided mirror is said to be enchanted with the darkest magic in Gnawnia. Its magic is so powerful that any mouse who dabbles in the dark arts cannot help themselves but to gaze eagerly into its mysterious panes.<br>Each majestic panel serves a unique purpose in the process of catching the unfortunate and unsuspecting mice who dare approach it. One mirror shows an unspoken truth to fill them with doubt. Another mirror shows them an alluring but unreachable place to fill them with longing. The final mirror shows them a magnified and distorted image of themselves to fill them with dread. As the three reflections converge, the mouse is driven nearly to madness and will seek refuge between the cracks in the mirrors. Once they venture beyond the glass, they disappear into a strange, shadowy realm never to return.<br>Hunters are recommended to avert their eyes and keep a safe distance before uttering the phrase: "Magic Mirrors on my base, vanish this mouse without a trace!" |  |  |  |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Draconic

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Draconic](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Draconic)

## Overview

Draconic is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Draconic power type is symbolized by an image of a dragon's wing. Image

Hunters can change their power type to Draconic by arming a Weapon with Draconic power type.

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Draconic traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

| Indigenous Mice<br>Dock Dwellers<br>Draconic Brood<br>Gauntlet Gladiators<br>The Marching Flame<br>Queso Canyoneers | Moussu Picchu Inhabitants<br>Floating Islanders<br>Dragons of the Depths<br>Rift Walkers<br>Event Mice |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Dragon Slayer Cannon

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Dragon Slayer Cannon](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Dragon_Slayer_Cannon)

The Dragon Slayer Cannon is a Draconic type weapon with special effect in Moussu Picchu.

| Dragon Slayer Cannon |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Draconic |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 10,000 | Title Required: | Grand Duke/Grand Duchess |
| Power Bonus: | 35% | Cost: | 18,000,000 gold310 Cork Bark15 Congealed Rubber30 Geyserite60 Tungsten2 Ember Root2 Ember Stone415 Dragon Scale1 Ful'Mina's Tooth1 Kalor'ignis Rib1 Chrome Storm Wrought Ballista Trap Parts |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 25 | Crafted: | Yes |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | Yes |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| A devastating machine created with an incredibly rare rib of a terrifying dragon, housed in a heat-resistant shell. It draws power from the Kalor'ignis Rib stored within, focusing it in its centre, building a giant bubble of Draconic energy that expands outwards. Once it has expanded beyond critical mass, the outer blades begin to spin at an incredible velocity, shaping the energy from spherical to cylindrical and finally to a sharpened point. Once the formed mass of energy comes into contact with the point of the firing pin, it explodes forwards in an awesome beam of Draconic energy.<br>Enhanced by dark dragon magic drawn from Ful'Mina's powerful tooth, its powerful energy can blast an additional Dragon Scale from the clutches of its victims. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Dragon Slayer Cannon can be purchased from the Queso Geyser Trapsmith for:

- 18,000,000 gold
- 310 Cork Bark
- 15 Congealed Rubber
- 30 Geyserite
- 60 Tungsten
- 2 Ember Root
- 2 Ember Stone
- 415 Dragon Scale
- 1 Ful'Mina's Tooth
- 1 Kalor'ignis Rib
- 1 Chrome Storm Wrought Ballista Trap Parts

It cannot be refunded.

The Dragon Slayer Cannon (if unintentionally smashed) can be reassembled from Dragon Slayer Cannon Trap Parts.

### Formula

| Reconstruction Formula: Rebuild Dragon Slayer Cannon |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Recipe can be unlocked by: | Capture any mouse using the Dragon Slayer Cannon |  |  |
| Item | Qty | Cost (Each) | Source |
| Dragon Slayer Cannon Trap Parts | 1 | 18,000,000 | Using the Hunter's Hammer to smash the Dragon Slayer Cannon |
| Total | 18,000,000 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

It can also be crafted given the proper ingredients through a series of discontinued research steps.

The process of crafting this weapon had 4 steps:

- 1: Obtain a Kalor'ignis Rib
- 2: Craft a Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis
- 3: Craft a Imbued Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis
- 4: Craft the Dragon Slayer Cannon

### Research (No longer required)

When a Hunter without a Kalor'ignis Rib and Dragon Slayer Cannon reaches the Queso Geyser Trapsmith, a message shows:

With the appearance of Kalor'ignis, one of the legendary Dragonic mice thought only to be a myth, the Trapsmith has been convinced that there remains an as of yet undiscovered and extremely powerful trap. She appears to be looking for an especially rare trap component that will help her unlock some of the mystery behind this trap.

Find this fabled rib, sourced from Kalor'ignis himself, and return it to the Trapsmith in order to help her discover its secrets.

The Weapon Stats and Rank requirement are partially shown as:

- Rank: Grand Duke/Grand Duchess
- Power Type: Draconic
- Power: ???
- Power Bonus: ???
- Attraction Bonus: ???
- Luck: ???

It also lists the first step of the research - Recover Kalor'ignis Rib. This is done by obtaining a Kalor'ignis Rib from Kalor'ignis of the Geyser or other sources such as Marketplace or Great Winter Hunt.

### Crafting Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis

After obtaining a Kalor'ignis Rib, the message changes to:

Upon showing the Kalor'ignis Rib to the Trapsmith, she excitedly leads you to a back room and reveals an ancient tapestry in a protective glass case. It looks incredibly old and severely worn.

In one corner of the tattered document, barely visible, you notice a shape that resembles the rib. This must be some sort of blueprint for a trap!

The fabric itself is a heavy material with thread made from precious metals intricately stitched through its surface. Detailed drawings and hardly legible text in a language you cannot decipher cover the surface of what barely remains of the ravaged blueprint.

After some very frantic gesturing at various obscured drawings and text, the Trapsmith shares her theory about how to construct the trap's initial frame out of materials capable of withstanding extreme pressure.

I should experiment with the items used in the construction of every cork type to see if I can put together this so-called chassis that the Trapsmith needs to begin her work...

More Weapon Stats and Title requirement are revealed:

- Rank: Grand Duke/Grand Duchess
- Power Type: Draconic
- Power: 10,000
- Power Bonus: ???
- Attraction Bonus: ???
- Luck: ???

It also lists the second step of the research - Construct a chassis. This is done by crafting the Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis.

#### Formula

| Formula: Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Item | Qty | Cost (Each) | Source |
| Cork Bark | 310 | 0 | Burly Bruiser, Cork Defender, Corky, the Collector, Corkataur, Fuzzy Drake, Horned Cork Hoarder, Rambunctious Rain Rumbler in Queso Geyser<br>It can be obtained from opening a Queso Canyon Grand Tour Treasure Chest.<br>It can be bought from King's Cart and King's Arms General Store for 2 King's Credits. |
| Congealed Rubber | 15 | 0 | Loot found in Dragon Nest from Small Eruption in Queso Geyser<br>Using Magic Nest Dust on Tiny and Small Eruption Dragon Nest in Queso Geyser |
| Geyserite | 30 | 0 | Loot found in Dragon Nest from Medium Eruption in Queso Geyser<br>Using Magic Nest Dust on Medium Eruption Dragon Nest in Queso Geyser |
| Tungsten | 60 | 0 | Loot found in Dragon Nest from Large Eruption in Queso Geyser<br>Using Magic Nest Dust on Large and Epic Eruption Dragon Nest in Queso Geyser |
| Tiny Platinum Bar | 300 | 60,000 (18,000,000 total) | Cursed City General Store<br>Furoma Rift General Store<br>Labyrinth General Store<br>Lost City General Store<br>Queso Geyser General Store<br>Training Grounds General Store<br>Zokor General Store |
| Total | 18,000,000 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

### Crafting Imbued Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis

After crafting the Chassis, the message changes to:

Before even revealing that you have the completed Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis, the Trapsmith waves you over and urges you into the black room.

She excitedly takes the chassis and looks it over thoroughly while also consulting the tapestry. After several minutes of incoherent mumbling and scrutinizing of the chassis under a blinding head-lamp, the Trapsmith returns the incomplete trap to you and points to the strange metallic text on the ancient fabric.

She reveals the text implies that the chassis must now become imbued with "An Impossibly Sharp Blade as Bright as it is Loud", some "Glowing Ore From Deep Below" and something that is "Endlessly Ablaze but Does Not Burn". That is all she is able to decipher from the text.

More Weapon Stats and Title requirement are revealed:

- Rank: Grand Duke/Grand Duchess
- Power Type: Draconic
- Power: 10,000
- Power Bonus: 35%
- Attraction Bonus: 10%
- Luck: ???

It also lists the second step of the research - Imbue the chassis. This is done by crafting the Imbued Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis.

#### Formula

| Formula: Imbued Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Item | Qty | Cost (Each) | Source |
| Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis | 1 | 18,000,000 | Combine 310 Cork Bark, 15 Congealed Rubber, 30 Geyserite, 60 Tungsten and 300 Tiny Platinum Bars on Crafting Table |
| Ful'Mina's Tooth | 1 | 0 | Dropped by Ful'Mina, The Mountain Queen in Moussu Picchu<br>Dropped by Avalancheus the Glacial, Incendarius the Unquenchable, Three'amat the Mother of Dragons, and Pestilentia the Putrid in Draconic Depths<br>Purchased from Moussu Picchu General Store<br>Found in Rare Moussu Picchu Treasure Chest |
| Ember Root | 2 | 0 | Inferna, The Engulfed<br>Various mice in Eruptions<br>Queso Canyon Grand Tour Treasure Chest |
| Ember Stone | 2 | 0 | Nachous, The Molten<br>Various mice in Eruptions<br>Queso Canyon Grand Tour Treasure Chest |
| Total | 18,000,000 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

### Crafting Dragon Slayer Cannon

After crafting the Imbued Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis, the message changes to:

As you finish imbuing the Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis, you notice that when you bring it close to the Kalor'ignis Rib, they seem to resonate with a dangerous energy.

Upon revealing this strange interaction to the wide-eyed Trapsmith, she states how imperative she believes it will be to combine the correct components in order to safely control its incredible energy and complete the trap.

Referring to the tattered tapestry, you and the Trapsmith theorize what the final components could be. The strange diagram seems to be referring to something that has the capability to redirect incredible amounts of power... Or is it perhaps suggesting that it requires a large number of items that can absorb energy?

The Trapsmith states that you'll need to conduct experiments involving the imbued chassis, the rib, and other Draconic equipment and supplies you've found along the way.

The complete Weapon Stats and Title requirement is displayed:

- Rank: Grand Duke/Grand Duchess
- Power Type: Draconic
- Power: 10,000
- Power Bonus: 35%
- Attraction Bonus: 10%
- Luck: 25

It also lists the last step of the research - Assemble the Cannon. This is done by crafting the Dragon Slayer Cannon.

#### Formula

| Formula: Dragon Slayer Cannon |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Item | Qty | Cost (Each) | Source |
| Imbued Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis | 1 | 18,000,000 | Combine 1 Dragon Slayer Cannon Chassis, 1 Ful'Mina's Tooth, 2 Ember Roots amd 2 Ember Stones on Crafting Table |
| Kalor'ignis Rib | 1 | 0 | Loot from Kalor'ignis of the Geyser in Queso Geyser<br>Marketplace |
| Chrome Storm Wrought Ballista Parts | 1 | 13,700,000 | Use the Hunter's Hammer to smash the Chrome Storm Wrought Ballista Trap |
| Dragon Scale | 415 | 0 | Loot drop by Storm Dragons in Moussu Picchu<br>The following Traps generate an additional Dragon Scale upon catching Storm Dragons in Moussu Picchu:<br>Storm Wrought Ballista Trap<br>Chrome Storm Wrought Ballista Trap<br>Dragon Slayer Cannon<br>Chrome Dragon Slayer Cannon<br>Dragon Devastator Multi-cannon |
| Total | 31,700,000 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

After crafting the Dragon Slayer Cannon, the message changes to:

Construction complete!
You've solved the mystery and assembled the cannon!

With the components shown in the ancient tapestry now assembled together, the Trapsmith looks at you with a smile and gives a quick cheer!

You both proudly stand over the trap, taking a moment to admire your hardwork and the beautiful piece of machinery you put together as a team, before shaking hands and parting ways.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Moussu Picchu
Generates an additional Dragon Scale when one is dropped as loot

# ⚔️ Weapon: Event Horizon Trap

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| A piece of cheese is placed upon a trigger at the exact event horizon of this trap. Any mouse caught lingering long enough for the machine to spin up completely will be caught within the artificial black hole's gravitational pull.<br>Once a mouse crosses the event horizon, the gravitational pull becomes so strong that escape becomes impossible!<br>Although, you won't really see this happen. As the mice pass through the horizon, they will appear to freeze in place as they fall infinitely into the center, slowly redshifting until they eventually disappear from sight.<br>The mouse, however, will experience gravity at a magnitude so strong that it will experience something known as "spaghettification". This is a very scientific term which means it will be stretched out into a long length of atoms, laid out side by side, resembling a string of spaghetti, pulled infinitely apart until the space between them is so great that only darkness remains.<br>At least... That's what is believed will happen. No one has ever gone in and returned to tell about it, or properly observed it. Perhaps a hunter will reach too close by mistake when placing their cheese and become an unwilling test subject. Their noble and honourable sacrifice will prove to be invaluable to the world of science. |  |  |  |

Normally powered by the magic of Father Winter, this version has been adapted to specifically target the Acolyte Mouse.<br>Vulnerable to those magics long forgotten, any Acolyte Mouse that is caught with this device is immediately drained of all power which is then used to increase the power of the trap itself!<br>The Chrono Mouse is the only one powerful enough to rewind the timepiece and release all of the stored Acolyte power from the trap.<br>The power from the timepiece was originally used to create toys, trinkets and other gifts as well as keep Father Winter on track to deliver all the presents. # ⚔️ Weapon: Forgotten

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Forgotten](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Forgotten)

## Overview

Forgotten is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Hunters can change their power type to Forgotten by arming either:

- A Weapon with Forgotten power type
- The Golem Guardian Forgotten Skin Module
- Forgotten Charm (this will not work if a power type-changing skin is armed)

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Forgotten traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

| --- | --- | --- |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Golem Guardian Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Golem Guardian Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Golem_Guardian_Trap)

The Golem Guardian Trap is a Limited Edition Weapon for which there are five Limited Edition Modules that change the base trap's stats and power type.

Depending on which skin the trap has equipped, the Golem Guardian Trap will take on a different Power Type. Each skin provides different maximum stats. All stats below are for a fully charged module.

Each hunt you take with the trap will increase its charge and as a result increase its Power, Luck, Power Bonus, and Attraction Bonus. It takes 500 hunts to fully charge a skin. Using a Golem Guardian Charm will charge the Golem Guardian Trap 10 times faster. Once fully charged, a unique golem will emerge from the trap signalling it has reached full strength.

Only one skin for the Golem Guardian Trap can be fully charged at a time. Upon changing the skin, each hunt taken with the Golem Guardian Trap will reduce the charge of all other skins which hold any charge while increasing the charge of the currently active skin. Charge levels will only increase or decrease while hunting with the Golem Guardian Trap and swapping skins will not reset charge.

| A specially modified Golem Generator designed to create a Golem Guardian for your cheese. This golem does not leave the generator, this is their generator and they have to defend it!<br>Since the golem is generated, it takes some time before the trap can fully power up. Every hunt you take will slowly generate specialized, weapons grade, animated snow to create your Golem Guardian. Once fully powered, your Golem will pop out and appear!<br>The Golem Guardian Trap supports special modules that change the configuration of the golem generated, changing its power type to match that of the module.<br>WARNING: If you change your Golem Guardian Trap Module, it will take time to re-assimilate the previously generated golems, slowly disassembling them to build a new one from the newly selected module. |  |  |  |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Hydro

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Hydro](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Hydro)

## Overview

Hydro is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Hunters can change their power type to Hydro by arming either:

- A Weapon with Hydro power type
- The Golem Guardian Hydro Skin Module or the Isle Idol Hydroplane Trap Skin
- Hydro Charm (this will not work if a power type-changing skin is armed)

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Hydro traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

| Indigenous Mice<br>Dock Dwellers<br>Mountain Mice<br>Forest Guild<br>Lab Experiments<br>Aquatic Order | The Elub Tribe<br>The Nerg Tribe<br>The Derr Tribe<br>Draconic Brood<br>Gauntlet Gladiators<br>Seasonal Soldiers | Icewing's Invasion<br>The Marching Flame<br>Living Garden Mice<br>Deep Sea Dwellers<br>Fungal Fiends<br>Floating Islanders | Rift Walkers<br>The Polluted<br>Event Mice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

Blueprint skins are applied to the weapon by crafting it with the smashed trap parts. Unlike removable trap skins, the process is irreversible and the trap cannot be used to complete a few specific tasks in Library Assignments. Weapons with blueprint skins appear on the Hunter's Profile as a different item from their original counterparts and cannot be smashed with the Hunter's Hammer.

| When the darkest magic in Gnawnia is reflected upon itself, it compounds and amplifies into a twisted terror that stretches into infinity.<br>Considered mad by many, the reckless Trapsmiths who designed this dangerously powerful contraption, took the enchanted panes of the Dark Magic Mirrors Trap and multiplied them using some dark and secretive form of alchemy. The panes were then cast into solid gold frames made from Golden Goose Eggs and bound together by the immense strength of a vine from the Bountiful Beanstalk. Finally, the largest pane was inlaid with a finely cut and polished Royal Ruby Bean, mainly for aesthetic flair.<br>Mice who approach this ornate, elaborate, and enchanted construction expect to see themselves but instead find that it is the infinity that stares back at them. Infinite reflections of infinite possibilities pierce the darkest recesses of their minds, enveloping and then consuming them, leaving nothing behind except a shadow without a source, persisting but not existing ad infinitum. |  |  |  |

The sheet of water rushing out of the bottom of this trap is powerful enough to knock over even the strongest of mice, so any mice attempting to steal cheese from this trap are advised to bring strong wading-poles and use the buddy system for safety.Any Elub mice washed away by the tide will also drop double the Seashells, which filter down through an ingenious collection system where they are sorted, categorized, and deposited directly into your inventory. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Isle Idol Hydroplane Trap Skin can be obtained by opening the Isle Idol Box Set, which was available to be purchased for 1,250,000 gold from Ronza's Traveling Shoppe during Ronza's March 2013 visit. The box could be refunded for 750,000 gold at the same location.

## Location-Specific Effects

The Isle Idol Hydroplane Trap Skinned Isle Idol Trap doubles the range of Seashell dropped in Elub Shore.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Law

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Law](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Law)

## Overview

Law is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Law power type is symbolized by an image of the scales of justice. Image

Hunters can change their power type to Law by arming a Weapon with Law power type.

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Law traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

| Indigenous Mice<br>Dock Dwellers<br>Mountain Mice<br>Forest Guild<br>Lab Experiments<br>Gauntlet Gladiators | Wild Bunch<br>Train Robbers<br>Meteorite Miners<br>Muridae Market Mice<br>Queso Canyoneers<br>Floating Islanders | Conclusion Cliffhangers<br>Rift Walkers<br>Event Mice |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Maniacal Brain Extractor

The Maniacal Brain Extractor is a Limited Edition Shadow type weapon. It is the upgraded version of the Brain Extractor.

| Maniacal Brain Extractor |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Shadow |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,600 | Title Required: | Lord |
| Power Bonus: | 10% | Cost: | 692,800 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 13 | Crafted: | Yes |
| Cheese Effect: | Very Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| The Brain Extractor, maniacal edition, has some pretty nifty upgraded features! First, introducing the power of two! Double the lights, double the straps, double the brain fluid storage cylinder = double the fun! Next, a high tech Newtonian laser makes sure that your lobotomies are level! Nothings worse than sophisticated surgery being botched by slopey slants. Works in tandem with the sonic corkscrew to level the device based on the lasers readout. Worried about your patients' comfort levels? The serum syringe administers high grade truth serum so you know your patients are definitely telling the truth when they say it doesn't hurt one bit! Last but not least, the automatic scrubber arm keeps your workstation neat and tidy before and after every procedure! Comes with the new iGor OS 7 upgrade for your droid overseer! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Maniacal Brain Extractor is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It was not sold by any Trapsmith. It can only be crafted given the proper ingredients.

| Invisi-glu | 20 | 50(1,000 total) | Bazaar General Store |
| King's Reserve Bubbleh | 1 | 210,000 | Great Gnarled Tree General Store<br>New Year's Mouse during Great Winter Hunt 2017 |
| Rope | 3 | 600(1,800 total) | Hydra mice in Lagoon and S.S. Huntington IV<br>Monstrous Black Widow mice in Whisker Woods Rift<br>Great Gnarled Tree General Store |
| Tiny Platinum Bar | 4 | 60,000(240,000 total) | Cursed City General Store<br>Furoma Rift General Store<br>Labyrinth General Store<br>Lost City General Store<br>Queso Geyser General Store<br>Training Grounds General Store<br>Zokor General Store |
| Total | 692,800 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

## Special Effects

- The Maniacal Brain Extractor collects Brain Bits from the various zombie mice. The effect is greater when paired with the Skello-ton Base.
- Spooky Bonus: The Maniacal Brain Extractor acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Spooky Charm variant.

## Related Items

- Super Brain Charm - Require Brain Bits to craft and increase the encounter rate of the various zombie mice.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Mystic Pawn Pincher

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Mystic Pawn Pincher](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Mystic_Pawn_Pincher)

The Mystic Pawn Pincher is a Tactical weapon with special effects in Zugzwang's Tower.

| Mystic Pawn Pincher |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Tactical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 60 | Title Required: | Lord/Lady |
| Power Bonus: | 5% | Cost: | 300,000 gold |
| Attraction Bonus: | 20% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 0 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| When Mystic Pawns see the glowing energies from this trap, they flock to it, hoping to top up their reserve. The Hunter must then whisper the forbidden words which direct the crane arm, positioning it over the hapless pawn before the allotted time passes. If the spell is said correctly, the runic claw drops down and nabs the Mystic Pawn. Technic Pawn armor is too slippery for the Arcane Crane, making them fall out of the claw before it gets a good grip. |  |  |  |

## Purchasing Info

The Mystic Pawn Pincher can be purchased from Trapsmith in Seasonal Garden and Zugzwang's Tower for 300,000 gold.

It can be refunded for 54,000 gold at the same locations.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Zugzwang's Tower
Attracts more Mystic Pawn and less Technic Pawn
Has a bonus of 10,920 Power and 51 Luck against Mystic Pawn
Has a penalty of 60 Power and 0.05 Luck against Technic Pawn

## Related Weapons

- Technic Pawn Pincher - is designed to catch the Technic Pawn while avoiding the Mystic Pawn
- Blackstone Pass Trap - has increased effectiveness against Mystic mice and decreased effectiveness against Technic mice
- Obvious Ambush Trap - has increased effectiveness against Technic mice and decreased effectiveness against Mystic mice

# ⚔️ Weapon: Nannybot

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Nannybot](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Nannybot)

The Nannybot is a Limited Edition Parental type weapon. It is the only Parental type weapon in the game.

| Nannybot |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Parental |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 525 | Title Required: | Novice |
| Power Bonus: | 5% | Cost: | 0 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 5 | Crafted: | Yes |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | Yes |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Straighten up and mind your manners: the Nannybot is on duty! She has camera 'eyes' in the back of her head, four extra claw arms that allow her to be absolutely everywhere at once, and the Time Out Engine, a device that will catch and hold mice frozen in time until the hunter is ready to deal with them. Some mice 'borrowed' this model from Digby scientists as a prototype, so it lacks all the features of a factory model. Maybe with a few supplies, you could fix it up. . . |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Nannybot is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It could be assembled from the loot dropped by various mice in the Town of Gnawnia during the MouseHunt Birthday II event.

- Nanny Bolts from a Birthday Mouse
- Time Out Hour Glass from a Present Mouse
- Intimidating Pincher from a Rockstar Mouse

Once all of the weapon components had been gathered, the Nannybot could be constructed on a special Nannybot assembly page (no longer available). It cannot be assembled initially via crafting.

After assembling the Nannybot, hunters see the following message:

The Nannybot can also be reassembled from Nannybot Parts.

### Formula

| Reconstruction Formula: Rebuild Nannybot |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Item | Qty | Cost (Each) | Source |
| Nannybot Parts | 1 | N/A | Use the Hunter's Hammer to smash the Nannybot trap |
| Total | 0 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

## Special Effects

- The Nannybot was the only weapon capable of catching the Terrible Twos Mouse until the Nanny Charm was introduced the following year.

Eventually, the Terrible Twos Mouse developed a weakness to all power types (except Rift). Currently, there are a fairly small number of mice in the game that have a weakness to Parental traps, but none that are exclusively susceptible to the Parental power type.

## Related Items

### Weapons

- Chrome Nannybot – The Nannybot can be upgraded into this Shadow trap with the Time Out Upgrade, Chrome Nanite Coating, and Superstar Nanny Glasses.
- A complete list of Parental weapons can be found here.

### Charms

- Nanny Charm - Converts any weapon's Power Type to Parental when armed.

## Hunter's Hammer

Smashing this weapon with the Hunter's Hammer yields the Nannybot Parts, used in crafting the Chrome Nannybot.

Because of the Technic King's passion for heavy artillery, Technic Mice have an irresistible compulsion to battle any strange technology and acquire it for the team. Even an obvious ambush brings Technics running in hopes that they can be the one to bring a new weapon home to the King. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Obvious Ambush Trap can be purchased from Trapsmith in Seasonal Garden or Zugzwang's Tower for:

- 650,000 gold
- 1 Droid Jetpack

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Zugzwang's Tower
Has a bonus of 1,800 Power and 6 Luck against Technic mice
Has a penalty of 2,400 Power and 9 Luck against Mystic mice

## Related Weapons

- Blackstone Pass Trap – has increased effectiveness against Mystic mice and decreased effectiveness against Technic mice
- Mystic Pawn Pincher - is designed to catch the Mystic Pawn in Zugzwang's Tower while avoiding the Technic Pawn
- Technic Pawn Pincher - is designed to catch the Technic Pawn in Zugzwang's Tower while avoiding the Mystic Pawn

# ⚔️ Weapon: Queso Factory Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Queso Factory Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Queso_Factory_Trap)

The Queso Factory Trap is a Law type weapon with a Location-Specific Effect in Queso River.

| Queso Factory Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Law |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,000 | Title Required: | Lord/Lady |
| Power Bonus: | 12% | Cost: | 117,0005 Golden Punch Ticket |
| Attraction Bonus: | 30% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 5 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Extremely Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| After a year of studying the SUPER brie+ Factory from the limited time they were allowed access, some clever Trapsmiths have created a smaller, mouse-sized version that can retrofit into the pumps in the Queso River and increase their efficiency!<br>While hunting in the Queso River, this trap taps into the flowing queso and extracts it based on your current pump level. Once it has reached maximum capacity, it adds 500 bonus Bland Queso to your next pump! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Queso Factory Trap is available during the annual MouseHunt Birthday event.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Queso River
After a certain number of catches, this weapon generates 500 Bland Queso.
The number of catches needed depends on the Pump level.

| Pump level | Number of catches |
| --- | --- |
| 1 | 100 |
| 2 | 50 |
| 3 | 34 |
| 4 | 25 |
| 5 | 20 |
| 6 | 17 |
| 7 | 15 |
| 8 | 13 |
| 9 | 12 |
| 10 | 10 |

## Special Effects

- Party Bonus: The Queso Factory Trap acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Party Charm variant.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Hydro |  |  |  |

The specially crafted spouts focus the eruption towards the placement of the cheese on the trap hoping to cover any mice that wander too close. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Queso Fount Trap can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Queso Geyser for:

- 12,000,000 Gold
- 500 Solidified Amber Queso
- 75 Nachore

It requires that the Hunter has completed the Slay the Geyser Dragon adventure prior to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Rift

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Rift](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Rift)

## Overview

Rift is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Rift power type is symbolized by an image of swirling mist. Image

Hunters can change their power type to Rift by arming a Weapon with Rift power type.

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Rift traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

## Rift Set Bonuses

Arming more than one Rift-type trap components of Weapon, Base, and Charm will enable a Set Bonus.

By default, doing so will activate the Riftwalker Set bonus. It can be upgrade into the Riftstalker Set with the purchase of Riftstalker Codex.

|  | Riftwalker Set | Riftstalker Set |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Set Bonus (2 Pieces) | +20% Power Bonus | +40% Power Bonus |
| Set Bonus (3 Pieces) | +20% Power Bonus<br>+5 Luck | +40% Power Bonus<br>+10 Luck |
| The Rift Set Bonuses require the Hunter to own a Rift Base, Weapon, and Charm. |  |  |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Rift Glacier Gatler

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Rift Glacier Gatler](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Rift_Glacier_Gatler)

The Rift Glacier Gatler is a Limited Edition Rift type weapon.

| Rift Glacier Gatler |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Rift |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 1,250 | Title Required: | Count/Countess |
| Power Bonus: | 10% | Cost: | 25,000 Gold40 Toboggan Sugar Cookie10 Nutcracker Sugar Cookie10 Ornament Sugar Cookie30 Snowflake Sugar Cookie |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 3 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Very Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Win every snowball fight across dimensions with the Rift Glacial Gatler! The most high-tech snowball-blasting machine there is!<br>Now, don't let the number of barrels fool you! These are state-of-the-art, A.I.-driven, Rocket-Propelled Mouse-Tracking Barrels! Quite the modern marvel in snowball fighting and mouse catching fusion technology!<br>Once approached, the turrets hum to life and launch from the gatler platform, using an A.I.-powered tracking technology derived from attack and dispersal patterns learned from thousands of snowball fights. Each turret can independently predict a mouse's path and fire at its anticipated position. If the prediction is off for any given turret, the 3 others pick up the slack and learn from its mistakes!<br>The concentrated atmosphere of the Rift adds to the already tried, tested and true mist-condensing technology, providing the chance to create a Rift Snowball Charm upon successfully capturing a mouse. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Rift Glacier Gatler could be purchased from the Trapsmith in Festive Comet during Great Winter Hunt 2019 for:

- 25,000 Gold
- 40 Toboggan Sugar Cookie
- 10 Nutcracker Sugar Cookie
- 10 Ornament Sugar Cookie
- 30 Snowflake Sugar Cookie

It cannot be refunded.

## Special Effects

- Adds Rift Snowball Charm to mouse loot pool
- Festive Bonus: The Rift Glacier Gatler acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Snowball Charm variant.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Ronza's Flock of Golden Anti-Dragonbots

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Ronza's Flock of Golden Anti-Dragonbots](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Ronza's_Flock_of_Golden_Anti-Dragonbots)

The Ronza's Flock of Golden Anti-Dragonbots is a Limited Edition Draconic type weapon.

| Ronza's Flock of Golden Anti-Dragonbots |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Draconic |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 5,700 | Title Required: | Recruit |
| Power Bonus: | 15% | Cost: | 50 Dragonhide Sliver |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 12 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Very Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| The year was 2024 when the great dragon invasion ascended from the Draconic Depths and took to the skies in a malevolent attempt to stop Ronza from aiding the MouseHunters of Gnawnia.<br>She had discovered the latest plot devised by the notorious Mythweaver and was on her way to the Kingdom to prepare them for the upcoming threat. However, as soon as her ship crossed into Gnawnian airspace, she was attacked by waves of dragons who had escaped the Draconic Depths and collected like-minded minions along their way. She was first attacked by Ice Dragons, followed by Poison Dragons, and finally a wave of ferocious Fire Dragons who bolstered their numbers by gathering their cousins from the Queso Geyser. At the sight of these horrid, winged beasts, there was a moment of utter despair as Ronza truly felt that all was lost.<br>But alas, just in the nick of time, the heroic MouseHunters of Gnawnia descended upon those sinister serpents in defence of their beloved Ronza! They traveled en masse from all corners of the known world to meet these formidable foes face-to-face and to show them the might, determination, and unity of the great Kingdom of Gnawnia. An epic battle ensued over the course of three long weeks where the skill and ingenuity of the MouseHunters was put to the test as they had to gather strange and exotic cheeses to lure these dastardly dragons away from Ronza's airship and directly into their traps. Along the way, the hunters were able to accumulate a curious collection of loot that had never yet been seen on the surface world.<br>The MouseHunters were at last victorious in their valiant efforts and thus Ronza was able to complete her mission. She successfully delivered the fabled Tale of the First Dragons that would lead the brave hunters onto their next, and possibly most dangerous, adventure into the Draconic Depths!<br>While the MouseHunters were bravely defending her airship, Ronza was afforded enough time to build a draconic defence of her own. She took all the gold that hunters had spent at her shoppe that year, melted it down, and used it to forge a fierce, fighting flock of Golden Anti-Dragonbots! Each Anti-Dragonbot was equipped with a fearsome laser beam that was powered by the mysterious Dragonhide Slivers that the attacking dragons had carried up with them from the Draconic Depths. With this new, state-of-the-art defence system, Ronza ensured that her flights would never again be troubled by the dreadful Dragons of the Depths.<br>As a heartfelt thank you to the MouseHunters who saved her, Ronza offered them all a parting gift that was an exact replica of this mighty new draconic defence system. They merely needed to provide the Dragonhide Slivers to power it and it was theirs to keep. As she flew off into the sunset once again, she bid them all a fond farewell and wished them the best of luck with their next daunting duty of descending into the dark and dismal den from which those dreadful, destructive dragons derived: the Draconic Depths!<br>Thus was the tale of Dragons' Ascent: The Battle for Ronza's Airship. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Ronza's Flock of Golden Anti-Dragonbots is available for purchase for 50 Dragonhide Sliver from Ronza's Shoppe.

It cannot be refunded.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Shadow

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Shadow](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Shadow)

## Overview

Shadow is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Hunters can change their power type to Shadow by arming either:

- A Weapon with Shadow power type
- Shadow Charm (this will not work if a power type-changing skin is armed)

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Shadow traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

It then uses its ultra-high powered laser tail to carve a slab out of the boulder so that it can carry the encased mouse and deliver it to the hunter on a stone platter.<br>It is said that the trap resonates a unique stonesong depending on the mouse but all the hunter ever hears is rock. # ⚔️ Weapon: Snowglobe Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Snowglobe Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Snowglobe_Trap)

The Snowglobe Trap is a Limited Edition Physical type weapon.

| Snowglobe Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Physical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,350 | Title Required: | Journeyman |
| Power Bonus: | 12% | Cost: | 341,000 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 16 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| It is a little-known fact that mice share humanity's obsession with the weather patterns of vinegar-filled miniature aquariums, which is, in part, why the Snowglobe trap is so successful. When a mouse comes near the snow globe to get a better look at the winter wonderland inside, the moving platform catapults it through the force field at the front of the globe, where it bonks its head on the the back wall. Once in the globe, it will freeze, becoming a natural part of the interior scenery. |  |  |  |

## Purchasing Info

The Snowglobe Trap is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It could be purchased for 341,000 gold from Ronza's Traveling Shoppe during The Great Winter Hunt '09 event. It could be refunded for 61,380 gold at the same location.

## Special Effects

- Festive Bonus: The Snowglobe Trap acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Snowball Charm variant.

## Related Items

- A complete list of Physical weapons can be found here.
- A complete list of Festive Weapons can be found here.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Soul Catcher

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Soul Catcher](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Soul_Catcher)

The Soul Catcher is a Limited Edition Shadow type weapon.

| Soul Catcher |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Shadow |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 1,500 | Title Required: | Master |
| Power Bonus: | 5% | Cost: | 190,000 gold1 Soulstone |
| Attraction Bonus: | 5% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 5 | Crafted: | Yes |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | Yes |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Harnessing the power of a Soulstone, this trap pierces the darkness and seeks out weaknesses in Shadow mice.<br>This shard of Soulstone pulsates slowly, as if longing to be alive. It almost seems to morph and bulge within its platinum setting as it hangs suspended in its web. Perhaps it can be improved if imbued with another powerful stone...<br>The haunting hum of the Soulstone gives this trap a luck bonus exclusively at the Gloomy Greenwood. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Soul Catcher is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It was available at the Trapsmith in the Haunted Terrortories for 190,000 gold plus 1 Soulstone during the Halloween at the Haunted Terrortories event. It could be refunded for 34,000 gold at the same location.

The Soul Catcher (if unintentionally smashed) can be reassembled from Soul Catcher Parts.

### Formula

| Reconstruction Formula: Rebuild Soul Catcher |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Recipe can be unlocked by: | Capture a mouse with the Soul Catcher |  |  |

| Soul Catcher Parts | 1 | 190,000 | Use the Hunter's Hammer to smash the Soul Catcher trap |
| Total | 190,000 gold |  |  |
| Recipe costs represent Shoppe prices only. The cost of items obtained through other sources will affect the total cost of crafting. |  |  |  |

## Location-Specific Effects

- Halloween event locations: has a bonus of 10 Luck
Haunted Terrortories
Gloomy Greenwood

## Special Effects

- Spooky Bonus: The Soul Catcher acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Spooky Charm variant.

## Hunter's Hammer

Smashing this weapon with the Hunter's Hammer yields Soul Catcher Parts, used in crafting the Soul Harvester.

Outfitted with a slew of clever traps it came as quite the surprise when the trapsmith who designed this tool of destruction found it utterly destroyed by it's own means, the statue untouched. It is believed the simple traps were insult enough to the spirit of this creature that they simply destroyed themselves in it's presence. |  |  |  |

The Sphynx Wrath can be purchased from Fiery Warpath Trapsmith for:

# ⚔️ Weapon: Supply Grabber

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Supply Grabber](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Supply_Grabber)

The Supply Grabber is a Law weapon designed to be used in the Supply Depot phase at the Gnawnian Express Station.

| Supply Grabber |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Law |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,000 | Title Required: | Lord/Lady |
| Power Bonus: | 10% | Cost: | 150,000 Gold30 Iron Pellet20 Copper Bead10 Tin Scrap |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 4 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| A once simple, coal-powered industrial shipment crane that has been modified to be able to snag mice carrying Royal Supply Crates at high speeds.<br>It fools mice with its slow and calculated movements until they come close, then it snaps into action and snatches them up!<br>This trap is very effective while the Gnawnian Express is at the Supply Depot. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Supply Grabber can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Gnawnian Express Station for:

- 150,000 Gold
- 30 Iron Pellet
- 20 Copper Bead
- 10 Tin Scrap

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Gnawnian Express Station
Provides an additional 1,500 Power in the Supply Depot phase

## Related Weapons

- Bandit Deflector - provides an additional 1,500 Power in the Raider River phase in the Gnawnian Express Station
- Engine Doubler - provides an additional 1,500 Power in the Daredevil Canyon phase in the Gnawnian Express Station
- The list of all Law weapons

The Surprise Party Trap is a Limited Edition Law type weapon.

| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |

| A birthday surprise party where the mice bring themselves right to the law enforcement! No need to serve fancy warrants or send elaborate SWAT (Special Weapons and Traps) teams! Just let the criminals walk right in!<br>Inside you will find a lovely Birthday Cake, a squad of droids who sing you your rights, a fun game of "put the cuffs on the criminal", surprise boxes of evidence, and some lucky mice even get to leave with a cool orange party jumpsuit!<br>This trap has a rare chance to drop a Party Charm when a mouse is caught! |  |  |  |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Tactical

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Tactical](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Tactical)

## Overview

Tactical is a Power Type used in MouseHunt.

Tactical power type is symbolized by an image of a stylized eye. Image

Hunters can change their power type to Tactical by arming either:

- A Weapon with Tactical power type
- The Isle Idol Stakeshooter Trap Skin or the Golem Guardian Tactical Skin Module

## Mice

Mice found in the following groups will have some weakness to Tactical traps, but these traps may not be the most effective. See individual Location and Mice pages for specific information.

| Indigenous Mice<br>Dock Dwellers<br>Mountain Mice<br>Forest Guild<br>Lab Experiments<br>The Shadow Clan | Digby Dirt Dwellers<br>Followers of Furoma<br>Aquatic Order<br>The Elub Tribe<br>The Nerg Tribe<br>The Derr Tribe | Draconic Brood<br>Gauntlet Gladiators<br>Seasonal Soldiers<br>Wizard's Pieces<br>Zurreal's Breed<br>The Marching Flame | Muridae Market Mice<br>Floating Islanders<br>Foreword Farmers<br>Rift Walkers<br>Event Mice |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

# ⚔️ Weapon: Technic Pawn Pincher

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Technic Pawn Pincher](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Technic_Pawn_Pincher)

The Technic Pawn Pincher is a Tactical weapon with special effects in Zugzwang's Tower.

| Technic Pawn Pincher |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Tactical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 60 | Title Required: | Lord/Lady |
| Power Bonus: | 5% | Cost: | 300,000 gold |
| Attraction Bonus: | 20% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 0 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Stale | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| With all of the gizmos, gadgets and mechanical gewgaws placed on the field by the Technic Team, one more coal engine isn't going to arouse much notice. In fact, this trap fits in so seamlessly with the Technic defensive line that Technic Pawns try to check it for repairs on a regular basis. When they do, the claw picks them up, and either flings them away, or drops them in the engine for three free hours of running time. Efficiency at its best! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Technic Pawn Pincher can be purchased from Trapsmith in Seasonal Garden and Zugzwang's Tower for 300,000 gold.

It can be refunded for 54,000 gold at the same locations.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Zugzwang's Tower
Attracts more Technic Pawn and less Mystic Pawn
Has a bonus of 10,920 Power and 51 Luck against Technic Pawn
Has a penalty of 60 Power and 0.05 Luck against Mystic Pawn

## Related Weapons

- Mystic Pawn Pincher - is designed to catch the Mystic Pawn while avoiding the Technic Pawn
- Blackstone Pass Trap - has increased effectiveness against Mystic mice and decreased effectiveness against Technic mice
- Obvious Ambush Trap - has increased effectiveness against Technic mice and decreased effectiveness against Mystic mice

# ⚔️ Weapon: Temporal Turbine

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Temporal Turbine](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Temporal_Turbine)

The Temporal Turbine is a Shadow type weapon.

| Temporal Turbine |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Shadow |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 5,000 | Title Required: | Duke/Duchess |
| Power Bonus: | 20% | Cost: | 10,500,000 Gold2 Temporal Shadow Plate1 Infused Plate225 Plate of Fealty |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 25 | Crafted: | Yes |
| Cheese Effect: | Stale | Smashable: | Yes |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Desolate and disheartening, this trap paints a picture of a bleak and meaningless existence, even when observed in its resting state. When a mouse approaches, the Temporal Turbine begins to hum, spinning violently and churning impossibly dark clouds, forming a direct link with the depths of a void in space beyond our plane of existence.<br>Once the mouse begins to gaze into the contraption, the turbine links with the mind of the mouse, implanting the image of a mouse utopia, convincing it to surrender itself as it astrally-projects the soul of the mouse into space, leaving behind a dormant husk, never to return. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Temporal Turbine can be purchased from Trapsmith in Labyrinth and Zokor for:

- 10,500,000 Gold
- 2 Temporal Shadow Plate
- 1 Infused Plate
- 225 Plate of Fealty

It cannot be refunded.

The Temporal Turbine (if unintentionally smashed) can be reassembled from Temporal Turbine Trap Parts.

# ⚔️ Weapon: The Haunted Manor Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [The Haunted Manor Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/The_Haunted_Manor_Trap)

The The Haunted Manor Trap is a Limited Edition Shadow type weapon.

| The Haunted Manor Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Shadow |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 3,500 | Title Required: | Lord |
| Power Bonus: | 10% | Cost: | 0 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 15 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Stale | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Legend has it that this was once a lavish home for sinister mice who eventually met a tragic end. It is believed that their dark spirits remain trapped within these walls for all eternity. These poltergeists now spend their cursed afterlife luring in others of their kind so that they may have company to share in their torment.<br>Hunters have been telling this ghost story around the campfire for years but no one really knows if there is any truth to it and anyone who has ever gone in to find out has never come out again. Most hunters are content to just let the trap do its job without asking too many questions.<br>Good luck finding a way out if you happen to get caught inside! You may come for a night, but you'll stay forever!<br>This trap has a rare chance to drop a Spooky Charm when a mouse is caught! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The The Haunted Manor Trap is a Limited Edition weapon and can only be acquired by Hunters during the Halloween 2020 event. It was not sold by any Trapsmith. It could only be obtained by collecting 800 Spooky Ship Parts during the 2020 Halloween event.

## Special Effects

- The The Haunted Manor Trap has a rare chance to drop a spooky charm when a mouse is caught.
- Haunted Estate Set: When combined with the Condemned Base, the usual Spooky Charm that can drop will be replaced by a Super Spooky Charm when one drops.
- Spooky Bonus: The The Haunted Manor Trap acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Spooky Charm variant.

## Related Weapons

- A complete list of Shadow weapons can be found here.
- A complete list of Halloween Weapons can be found here.

## Skins

- The Sugar Shack Trap Skin - Limited Edition

# ⚔️ Weapon: Thought Manipulator Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Thought Manipulator Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Thought_Manipulator_Trap)

The Thought Manipulator Trap is a Forgotten type weapon.

| Thought Manipulator Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Forgotten |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 12,112 | Title Required: | Archduke/Archduchess |
| Power Bonus: | 15% | Cost: | 10,700,000 Gold3 Sky Sprocket3 Skysoft Silk3 Enchanted Wing3 Cloudstone Bangle |
| Attraction Bonus: | 15% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 12 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| This upright stone formation stands inconspicuously awaiting a weary wanderer. While an unaware mouse sits at the base to rest and nibble on the cheese, the two jewels overhead silently activate. The first peers into the mind of the mouse to determine its innermost wants and desires while the second projects those desires onto the keyway beneath. Upon seeing the irresistible illusion, the mouse is compelled to step through the keyway into oblivion, never to return.<br>No one actually knows where the mice go but it is said that after stepping through, they are completely forgotten from history, as though they had never existed at all. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Thought Manipulator Trap can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Floating Islands for:

- 10,700,000 Gold
- 3 Sky Sprocket
- 3 Skysoft Silk
- 3 Enchanted Wing
- 3 Cloudstone Bangle

It cannot be refunded.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Thought Obliterator Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Thought Obliterator Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Thought_Obliterator_Trap)

The Thought Obliterator Trap is a Forgotten type weapon.

| Thought Obliterator Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Forgotten |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 14,000 | Title Required: | Archduke/Archduchess |
| Power Bonus: | 25% | Cost: | 21,000,000 Gold3 Sky Sprocket3 Skysoft Silk3 Enchanted Wing3 Cloudstone Bangle30 Adorned Empyrean Jewel |
| Attraction Bonus: | 15% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 18 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Extremely Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| This mystical stone structure possesses two immensely powerful jewels that actively seek out the thoughts of mice in the vicinity before they even become aware of it. The first of the two jewels draws the attention of unsuspecting mice with a projection of their desires directly into their mind. The images, sounds, and smells that they experience are completely indistinguishable from reality. The mice quickly become deeply entranced by this falsehood which lowers their mental guard and allows the second jewel to dig into their mind and erase its contents entirely. After forgetting who and where they are, they have no choice but to explore the attractive glowing keyway in front of them.<br>Once they pass through, they are erased from existence. Although, it has been theorized that the memory of their life and personality continues to exist trapped within the jewels for all eternity. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Thought Obliterator Trap can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Floating Islands for:

- 21,000,000 Gold
- 3 Sky Sprocket
- 3 Skysoft Silk
- 3 Enchanted Wing
- 3 Cloudstone Bangle
- 30 Adorned Empyrean Jewel

It requires the Hunter to own the Thought Manipulator Trap to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

The Thought Obliterator Trap (if unintentionally smashed) can be reassembled from Thought Obliterator Parts.
