# ⚔️ Weapon: Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Ultra_MegaMouser_MechaBot_Trap)

The Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap is a Limited Edition Physical type weapon.

| Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Power Type: Physical |  |  |  |
| Weapon Statistics | Weapon Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,250 | Title Required: | Novice |
| Power Bonus: | 5% | Cost: | 1 Repurposed Robot Head10 Blue Leg Robot Scraps10 Green Arm Robot Scraps10 Pink Leg Robot Scraps10 Red Chest Robot Scraps10 Yellow Arm Robot Scraps |
| Attraction Bonus: | 15% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 8 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Traditionally, these super-robots are formed of smaller robots that link together in times of great need, but when you build the whole thing from scraps all at once, you kind of limit your options. Plus, look at the size of the thing! When you work on a scale of that magnitude, you run into special engineering constraints that mean you have to make trade-offs: Either you can link together from smaller robots, or you can have your super robot stay together for more than one impact.Unfortunately, due to its massive size, the Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap isn't terribly effective against most mice. Mice are swift and hard to track, and the Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap is slow and ponderous. Still, against other super-robots, it's pretty swell! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap is a Limited Edition weapon and can no longer be acquired by Hunters. It could be purchased for 10 Pink Leg Robot Scraps, 10 Blue Leg Robot Scraps, 10 Green Arm Robot Scraps, 10 Yellow Arm Robot Scraps, 10 Red Chest Robot Scraps, and 1 Repurposed Robot Head. It could not be refunded.

The Repurposed Robot Head could be purchased from Ronza's Traveling Shoppe General Store for 100,000 gold.

## Special Effects

- Party Bonus: The Ultra MegaMouser MechaBot Trap acquires a 20% Power Bonus when armed with a Party Charm variant.

# ⚔️ Weapon: Weapons

> **Type**: Weapons | **Category**: Weapons | **Source**: [Weapons](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Weapons)

## Overview

A trap consists of two compulsory elements – one Weapon plus one Base. Charms, which enhance certain features of a trap, can optionally be armed by hunters ranked Journeyman/Journeywoman and above.

The Weapon is the part of the trap that determines (with very few exceptions) the Power Type of the trap. Weapons can combine with bases and charms to create specific effects for certain areas, for certain mice, or for certain loot. When Hunters begin working for the King they can choose to start hunting with either the High Tension Spring or the Tacky Glue Trap (Mouse Hot Tub if starting the game through the Mobile app). These are the most basic weapons available. As hunters capture more mice and earn more gold and experience, they will need to upgrade their traps with new weapons to increase their success in catching more powerful mice.

### Obtaining Weapons

Weapons can be obtained in the following ways (not all weapons are available via all methods):

- Purchased at a Trapsmith
- Crafted using a combination of Blueprints, looted trap parts, and other items.
- Obtained as loot

Weapons cannot be traded with other players or sold on the Marketplace, except for the Creepy Coffin Trap and the Shrink Ray Trap.

Limited Edition Weapons, as with all limited edition items, cannot be obtained once the original opportunity for acquiring them has ended.

### Using Weapons

A hunter's weapons, bases, and charms appear in the Traps Inventory tab. A hunter can select which trap component is armed from the inventory page, from the primary Camp page, and also from a Trapsmith / Charm Shoppe just after purchasing a trap component. Additionally, each Hunter's Profile includes a status of how many trap components a hunter owns, split into lists of weapons and bases on the Items tab.

It is not possible to hunt without a weapon armed.

### Weapon stats

- Power

- Power Type

- Power Bonus

- Attraction Bonus

- Luck

- Cheese Effect

- Special Effects

## Power Type

The Power Type of a trap set-up is determined by the weapon chosen by the Hunter. A weapon's power type determines its overall effectiveness when targeting Mice. Mice have weaknesses to specific power types, so Hunters must match the weapon with their mouse target for maximum effect.

A weapon's Power Type may be altered by arming Power Type Charms. The change will last only as long as the charms remain armed.

There are currently 10 different power types in the game. The list of Weapons for each power type can be found on the individual power type pages.

- Arcane
- Draconic
- Forgotten
- Hydro
- Law
- Parental
- Physical
- Rift
- Shadow
- Tactical

# 🛡️ Base: Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base

> **Type**: Bases | **Category**: Bases | **Source**: [Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Adorned_Empyrean_Refractor_Base)

| Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 1500 | Rank requirement: | Archduke/Archduchess |
| Power Bonus: | 20% | Cost: | 27,000,000 Gold200 Sky Ore 200 Sky Glass1 Adorned Empyrean Jewel |
| Attraction Bonus: | 15% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 20 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| This ornate, expertly crafted base was built to harness the full potential of the Adorned Empyrean Jewel by refracting its awesome power through many intricate, finely tuned facets and focusing it into the trap above. It was designed by the greatest lapidary ever to roam the clouds. The craftsman's name has unfortunately been lost to the ages but their work lives on as a powerful tool to rid the skies of vermin.<br>In addition to its extraordinary mouse-catching power, this miracle of modern science has the following special effect:<br>Floating Islands: Chance to add +1 to area-specific loot drops |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Floating Islands for:

- 27,000,000 Gold
- 200 Sky Ore
- 200 Sky Glass
- 1 Adorned Empyrean Jewel

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Floating Islands
Provides a chance to increase any area-specific loot drop by 1 if dropped by the mouse on a successful catch
Area-specific loot list includes:
Adorned Empyrean Jewel
Bottled Wind
Cloud Curd
Cloudstone Bangle
Corsair's Curd
Cyclone Stone
Empyrean Seal
Empyrean Treasure Trove
Enchanted Wing
High Altitude Treasure Trove
Low Altitude Treasure Trove
Raw Ancient Jade
Sky Glass
Sky Ore
Sky Pirate Seal
Sky Sprocket
Skysoft Silk
Storm Cell
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:
My Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base focused the light from my Skyfarer's Oculus and discovered the following loot:
1 <item X>
1 <item Y> (additional loot discovery continues on if the chance activates for more than 1 type of loot drop)

## Related Bases

- Royal Ruby Refractor Base - requires this base to purchase and has the same Floating Islands effect
- Sorcerer's Sapphire Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands effect
- Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands effect
- Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands effect

## Obtained Via

This base is available during the annual Halloween event:

- 2021: from the Reward Track after brewing 10 batches of Evil Extract.
- 2022: purchased in the Gloomy Greenwood Trapsmith for 450,000 gold.
- 2023: purchased in the Gloomy Greenwood Trapsmith for 450,000 gold.

# 🛡️ Base: Ancient Booster Base

> **Type**: Bases | **Category**: Bases | **Source**: [Ancient Booster Base](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Ancient_Booster_Base)

| Ancient Booster Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 300 | Rank requirement: | Initiate |
| Power Bonus: | 0% | Cost: | 0 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 10 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Built from the same jade and gold materials, this magical base shares an affinity with Ancient Relics. Upon contact with a Relic Hunter Mouse, this base will attempt to consume one Ancient Relic to generate extra Ancient Charms. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Ancient Booster Base is a Limited Edition base not sold by any Trapsmith. It may only be obtained from opening a Season 5 Treasure Chest.

Each Season 5 Relic Hunter Treasure Chest has a chance of containing the Ancient Booster Base. The more Season 5 chests you open, the greater your chance of finding the Limited Edition trap will be! After opening 29 chests, you're guaranteed to find the trap in your 30th if you have not already found it.

## Specific Effects

Upon capturing a Relic Hunter Mouse with the Ancient Booster Base, it will release the magic of an Ancient Relic from your inventory, crumbling into a pile of one of the four varieties of Ancient Charms. This process will consume an Ancient Relic from the Hunter's inventory. If a Hunter does not have any Ancient Relics, the base will have no effect.

| A tightly woven web of widow silk wrapped around itself, sticky enough that it can grab some bonus loot from the Black Widow Mouse when caught.<br>Using this base will reward bonus loot when a Black Widow Mouse is caught. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Black Widow Base is a Limited Edition base not sold by any Trapsmith. It may only be obtained from opening a Season 6 Treasure Chest.

Each Season 6 Relic Hunter Treasure Chest has a chance of containing the Black Widow Base. The more Season 6 chests you open, the greater your chance of finding the Limited Edition base will be!
There are two Limited Edition bases in Season 6, After opening 29 chests, you're guaranteed to either 
1) find both bases in your 30th (if it is a rare chest) if you have not already found them
2) find the first base in your 30th and the second base in your 31st (if it is a normal chest) if you have not already found them.

## Specific Effects

Upon capturing a Black Widow Mouse with the Black Widow Base, it adds bonus loot to the Black Widow Mouse including Magic Essence, Empowered SUPER|brie+, and Shadow Charms.

This base is extremely rich and very difficult to eat but that won't stop the mice from trying!<br>A single piece of Chocolatonium this large has the benefit of attracting the most chocolate-obsessed mice that are carrying the highest quantities of Chocolatonium during the Spring Egg Hunt.<br>This remarkable and delicious creation also occasionally provides a unique advantage to Eggstra Fondue. Consumes 5 Solidified Amber Queso per hunt
Generates a random item per catch from the list below:
Baitkeep Charm
Cork Bark
Dragonbane Charm
Ember Charm
Ember Root
Ember Stone
Flamin' Spice Leaf
Geyser Smolder Stone
Hot Spice Leaf
Kalor'ignis Rib
Magic Essence
Magic Nest Dust
Medium Spice Leaf
Mild Spice Leaf
Queso Thermal Spring
Super Dragonbane Charm
Thermal Chisel
Ultimate Charm
Ultimate Power Charm
Wild Tonic
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:
My Chrome Queso Cannonstorm Base blasted 5 Solidified Amber Queso to smithereens, revealing 1 <item X> within the rubble!
I have <n> Solidified Amber Queso left.
When the effect fails to activate, it generates a journal entry:
My Chrome Queso Cannonstorm Base blasted 5 Solidified Amber Queso but nothing was found within the rubble.
I have <n> Solidified Amber Queso left.

## Related Bases

- Chrome Base - Required to purchase
- Queso Cannonstorm Base - Original version

# 🛡️ Base: Chrome Stormcaster Base

> **Type**: Bases | **Category**: Bases | **Source**: [Chrome Stormcaster Base](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Chrome_Stormcaster_Base)

The Chrome Stormcaster Base is a base.

| Chrome Stormcaster Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 1250 | Rank requirement: | Archduke |
| Power Bonus: | 10% | Cost: | #Obtained Via |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 25 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Two different important bases were once built specifically for use at Moussu Picchu. The first, known as the Forecaster Base, was originally a sophisticated weather forecasting device that had the "unexpected feature" of actually intensifying the weather instead of merely predicting it. The second, known as the Storm Condenser Base, was a device constructed from repurposed hailstone that had a powerful coil and fan capable of capturing and neutralizing stormy weather.<br>Both of these miraculous base designs were lost to the Trapsmiths of Gnawnia... until now! It turns out that the villainous Captain Crook discovered and then stole these lost blueprints during her raid of Moussu Picchu so that she could make her own pirated versions. However, she got the blueprints for each all mixed up and couldn't tell them apart so she combined them into something new that has the effects of both!<br>Now that the design secrets have been recovered, the Trapsmith at the Cerulean Skyport is able to forge this new, stronger, and shinier version using the miraculous combination of Cerulean Chrome that has been charged with the energy from Adorned Empyrean Jewels!<br>While hunting in Moussu Picchu, hunters will be able to switch this base to one of the following modes:<br>Forecasting Mode:<br>Doubles the weather intensity gained on every successful catch based on the type of cheese that is armed<br>Condensing Mode:<br>Prevents the weather intensity from increasing |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Chrome Stormcaster Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Cerulean Skyport for:

- 5,000,000 Gold
- 20 Chrome Stormcaster Blueprint Scrap
- 200 Cerulean Chrome Ingot
- 3 Adorned Empyrean Jewel

It requires the Hunter to own the Chrome Base and complete the Liberate the Cerulean Skyport from Captain Crook adventure to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Moussu Picchu
Activate Forecasting Mode to double weather intensity gained on every catch based on cheese armed
Activate Condensing Mode to prevent the weather intensity from increasing

# 🛡️ Base: Chrome Thief Base

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| This remarkable base was originally designed by a group of clever yet morally-dubious hunters who pooled their knowledge and resources to create a base that could properly pillage the city of Zokor.<br>Some of them preferred a slow and quiet approach whereas others just wanted to smash and grab. After a lengthy debate, they finally decided: "Why not both?" And thus, the first truly switchable base was created.<br>However, the original design blueprints that contained the secrets to this base's extraordinary effects was lost to the Trapsmiths of Gnawnia... until now! It turns out that the villainous Captain Crook discovered and then stole these lost blueprints during her raid of Zokor so that she could make her own pirated version. Now that the design secrets have been recovered, the Trapsmith at the Cerulean Skyport is able to forge this new, stronger, and shinier version using the miraculous combination of Cerulean Chrome that has been charged with the energy from Adorned Empyrean Jewels!<br>Upon entering Zokor, hunters will be able to switch this base to one of the following modes:<br>Stealth Mode:<br>Provides a 50% chance to not lose Stealth.<br>Heist Mode:<br>Doubles Stealth lost but also doubles Fealty, Tech, and Scholar district loot gained.<br>This base does not affect loot drops in the Minotaur's Lair. |  |  |  |

Heist Mode:
Doubles Stealth lost
Doubles Plate of Fealty, Tech Power Core, Scholar Scroll, and Cavern Fungus dropped from mice

25% Power Bonus
25% Attraction Bonus
20 Luck

| Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 3,000 | Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Power Bonus: | 20% | Cost: | 24,000,000 gold1 Mythical Dragon Heart20,000 Dragon's Breath Opal |
| Attraction Bonus: | 50% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 35 | Crafted: | No |

| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Extraordinary advancements in energy refraction technology have allowed the Trapsmith at the Draconic Depths to create this exceptional new iteration in the line of refractor bases. By harnessing the enormous energy of the Mythical Dragon Heart and focussing it through one massive Dragon's Breath Opal, this base is capable of delivering a power so tremendous and terrifying that it rivals even the mightiest of dragons.<br>In addition to its extraordinary mouse-catching power, this miracle of modern science has the following special effects:<br>Floating Islands: Chance to add +1 to area-specific loot drops<br>Bountiful Beanstalk: Doubles Fabled Fertilizer loot drops<br>School of Sorcery: Adds +1 Spell Force<br>Doubles Dragonhide Sliver loot drops (Draconic Depths: Replicart™ compatible!) |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Draconic Depths for:

- 24,000,000 gold
- 1 Mythical Dragon Heart
- 20,000 Dragon's Breath Opal

It requires the Hunter to own the Sorcerer's Sapphire Refractor Base and complete the Vanquish the Mythical Dragon Emperor adventure to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

## Special Effects

When armed, the Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base doubles all Dragonhide Sliver loot drops in any location.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Floating Islands
Provides a chance to increase any area-specific loot drop by 1 if dropped by the mouse on a successful catch
Area-specific loot list includes:
Adorned Empyrean Jewel
Bottled Wind
Cloud Curd
Cloudstone Bangle
Corsair's Curd
Cyclone Stone
Empyrean Seal
Empyrean Treasure Trove
Enchanted Wing
High Altitude Treasure Trove
Low Altitude Treasure Trove
Raw Ancient Jade
Sky Glass
Sky Ore
Sky Pirate Seal
Sky Sprocket
Skysoft Silk
Storm Cell
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:
My Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base focused the light from my Skyfarer's Oculus and discovered the following loot:
1 <item X>
1 <item Y> (additional loot discovery continues on if the chance activates for more than 1 type of loot drop per item source)
- Bountiful Beanstalk
Doubles Fabled Fertilizer loot drops
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:
My Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base refracted and amplified the sunlight cultivating [X] additional Fabled Fertilizer!
- School of Sorcery
Adds +1 Spell Force
- Draconic Depths
Doubles Dragonhide Sliver loot drops

## Related Bases

- Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands effect
- Royal Ruby Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands and Bountiful Beanstalk effect

- Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base - requires this base to purchase and has the same Floating Islands, Bountiful Beanstalk, School of Sorcery, and Draconic Depths effect

| Luck: | 8 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Very Stale | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| Composed of a solar panel, battery pack, and strange, almost alien technology, this base has the ability to attract chunks of meteorite after charging up enough energy. It only seems to activate when the sun goes down and the charging cycle has completed, causing the glyphs etched into its surface to glow, and something from deep within begins to hum...<br>Use this base during the night in Fort Rox for a chance to find Meteorite Pieces. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Electromagnetic Meteorite Base is a Limited Edition base not sold by any Trapsmith. It may only be obtained from opening a Season 7 Treasure Chest.

Each Season 7 Relic Hunter Treasure Chest has a chance of containing the Electromagnetic Meteorite Base. The more Season 7 chests you open, the greater your chance of finding the Limited Edition base will be!
There are two Limited Edition bases in Season 7, After opening 29 chests, you're guaranteed to either 
1) find both bases in your 30th (if it is a rare chest) if you have not already found them
2) find the first base in your 30th and the second base in your 31st (if it is a normal chest) if you have not already found them.

## Specific Effects

Using this base during the Night in Fort Rox gives the chance of finding 1 to 3 Meteorite Pieces.

The Forecaster Base is a Limited Edition base not sold by any Trapsmith. It may only be obtained from opening a Season 8 Treasure Chest.

The Hunter's 30th Season 8 chest is guaranteed to contain the Forecaster Base if it is not yet owned by the Hunter.

## Obtained Via

The Furoma Base is a Limited Edition base not sold by any Trapsmith. It may only be obtained from opening a Season 6 Treasure Chest.

Each Season 6 Relic Hunter Treasure Chest has a chance of containing the Furoma Base. The more Season 6 chests you open, the greater your chance of finding the Limited Edition base will be!
There are two Limited Edition bases in Season 6, After opening 29 chests, you're guaranteed to either 
1) find both bases in your 30th (if it is a rare chest) if you have not already found them

It requires the Hunter to own Magnet Base, Spiked Base, Remote Detonator Base, Hearthstone Base, and Deep Freeze Base to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Iceberg
Increases the attraction rate of the Tunnel Rat mice
Prevents being pushed back by the Brute mice
Advances 20 feet upon catching a Bomb Squad mouse
Decreases the attraction rate of Zealot mice
Moves the Hunter to Hidden Depths upon catching Icewing
Has a bonus of 665 Power and 9 Luck from 1,800 feet onward

## Related Bases

- Magnet Base
- Spiked Base
- Remote Detonator Base
- Hearthstone Base
- Deep Freeze Base
- Ultimate Iceberg Base

| Wild Growth | 750 | 18% | 17 | 25% |

The Polluted Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Toxic Spill for:

- 1,434,000 Gold

- Has additional 4 Luck when armed with Polluted Charm
- Has additional 6 Luck when armed with Super Polluted Charm

- Has additional 15 Luck when armed with Ultimate Polluted Charm

Smashing this base with the Hunter's Hammer yields Polluted Base Parts.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| A slab of the foundation from the Valour Rift Gauntlet Tower. It shares a bond with the original structure and grows stronger the further you progress into the tower. The bond is so strong that it persists through and outside of the Rift Plane, in all other areas of the Kingdom.<br>This base will grow in power as the hunter reaches new heights:<br>+20 Power Boost per floor reached during Ultimate Umbra<br>+2 Luck Boost per Total Eclipse floor reached during Ultimate Umbra<br>These boosts are based on highest floor reached and are applied permanently as you reach greater heights during Ultimate Umbra. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Prestige Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Valour Rift for:

- 12,170,000 gold
- 2 Core of the Eclipse

The Hunter must have completed the following adventures in order to purchase the base:

- Capture the Eclipse Mouse
- Defeat Twisted Carmine
- Capture the Goliath Field Mouse
- Defeat the Menace of the Rift
- Capture the Monstrous Black Widow
- Capture the Grand Master of the Dojo
- Capture the Absolute Acolyte

It cannot be refunded.

## Special Effects

Whenever the Hunter reaches their highest Ultimate Umbra Floor, the additional stats are immediately applied to their Prestige Base:

- + 20 Power Boost per Floor reached
- + 2 Luck Boost per Eclipse Floor reached

| This repurposed Hailstone Cannon has been permanently set in a bed of hardened amber queso making it portable and allowing it to be used outside of the Great Winter Taiga. When used in the Queso Canyon region, this augmented cannon fires Solidified Amber Queso at any mouse that approaches your trap and each successful hit will explode into bonus Queso Canyon loot!<br><br>While armed within the Queso Canyon Region:<br>• This base will destroy 5 Solidified Amber Queso on every hunt<br>• Bonus Queso Canyon loot will be found in the rubble after catching a mouse |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

During Great Winter Hunt 2022, Queso Cannonstorm Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Cinnamon Hill, Golem Workshop, and Ice Fortress for:

- 3 Frost Globe

It requires Defeat the Frost King adventure to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Queso Canyon, Cerulean Skyport while intercepting Queso Geyser Raid
Consumes 5 Solidified Amber Queso per hunt
Generates a random item per catch from the list below:
Baitkeep Charm
Cork Bark
Dragonbane Charm
Ember Charm
Ember Root
Ember Stone
Flamin' Spice Leaf
Geyser Smolder Stone
Hot Spice Leaf
Kalor'ignis Rib
Magic Essence
Magic Nest Dust
Medium Spice Leaf
Mild Spice Leaf
Queso Thermal Spring
Super Dragonbane Charm
Thermal Chisel
Ultimate Charm
Ultimate Power Charm
Wild Tonic
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:
My Queso Cannonstorm Base blasted 5 Solidified Amber Queso to smithereens, revealing 1 <item X> within the rubble!
I have <n> Solidified Amber Queso left.
When the effect fails to activate, it generates a journal entry:
My Queso Cannonstorm Base blasted 5 Solidified Amber Queso but nothing was found within the rubble.
I have <n> Solidified Amber Queso left.

# 🛡️ Base: Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base

> **Type**: Bases | **Category**: Bases | **Source**: [Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Rainbow_Diamond_Refractor_Base)

The Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base is a base.

| Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 3,500 | Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Power Bonus: | 20% | Cost: | 35,000,000 gold225 Mythical Rainbow Diamond |
| Attraction Bonus: | 50% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 40 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Uber Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| This awe-inspiring, breathtakingly beautiful base represents the pinnacle of energy refraction technology in Gnawnia. After rigorously studying all the previous refractor base iterations, the Trapsmith at the Conclusion Cliffs has finally perfected this extraordinary technology by incorporating the incomparably powerful Mythical Rainbow Diamonds into its design. These miraculous minerals are able to bend, split, and concentrate trap energy in profoundly powerful ways and when combined in large quantities, they become more than the sum of their parts. All of the transformative power that has fueled the Mythweaver's nefarious efforts throughout the Folklore Forest has finally been captured, harnessed, and given a noble purpose with this monumental piece of mouse-catching equipment.<br>In addition to its extraordinary mouse-catching power, this miracle of modern science has the following special effects:<br>Floating Islands: Chance to add +1 to area-specific loot drops<br>Bountiful Beanstalk: Doubles Fabled Fertilizer loot drops<br>School of Sorcery: Adds +1 Spell Force<br>Draconic Depths: Doubles Dragonhide Sliver loot drops (Replicart™ compatible!)<br>Afterword Acres: Provides a chance to prevent the Blight from regrowing (stacks with the Rainbow Quill)<br>Epilogue Falls: Provides a chance to prevent getting pushed back by the current after failing to catch (stacks with the Rainbow Quill)<br>Conclusion Cliffs: Provides a chance to not lose a Postscript hunt after failing to catch (stacks with the Rainbow Quill)<br>Everywhere: Provides a chance to roll up armed Ultimate Ancient Charms into Rainbow Charms<br>Everywhere: Provides a chance for any tier of armed Rainbow Charm to roll up into their next, more powerful version |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Rainbow Diamond Refractor Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Conclusion Cliffs for:

- 35,000,000 gold
- 225 Mythical Rainbow Diamond

It requires the Hunter to own the Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base and complete the Conclude the Mythweaver's Story adventure to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

All previous refractor base effect and

- Afterword Acres: Provides a chance to prevent the Blight from regrowing (stacks with the Rainbow Quill)
- Bountiful Beanstalk: Doubles Fabled Fertilizer loot drops
- Conclusion Cliffs: Provides a chance to not lose a Postscript hunt after failing to catch (stacks with the Rainbow Quill)
- Draconic Depths: Doubles Dragonhide Sliver loot drops in Journal and in Replicart™
- Epilogue Falls: Provides a chance to prevent getting pushed back by the current after failing to catch (stacks with the Rainbow Quill)
- Floating Islands: Chance to add +1 to area-specific loot drops
- School of Sorcery: Adds +1 Spell Force
- Everywhere:
Provides a chance to roll up an armed Ultimate Ancient Charm into Rainbow Charm
Provides a chance for any tier of armed Rainbow Charm to roll up into their next, more powerful version
An armed Rainbow Charm rolls up to Rainbow Spore Charm
An armed Rainbow Spore Charm rolls up to Super Rainbow Spore Charm

## Related Bases

- Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands effect
- Royal Ruby Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands and Bountiful Beanstalk effect
- Sorcerer's Sapphire Refractor Base - has the same Floating Islands, Bountiful Beanstalk, and School of Sorcery effect
- Dragon's Breath Opal Refractor Base - requires this base to purchase and has the same Floating Islands, Bountiful Beanstalk, School of Sorcery, and Draconic Depths effect

# 🛡️ Base: Rift Hailstone Singularity Base

> **Type**: Bases | **Category**: Bases | **Source**: [Rift Hailstone Singularity Base](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Rift_Hailstone_Singularity_Base)

The Rift Hailstone Singularity Base is a Base that can gain additional stats.

| Rift Hailstone Singularity Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 490 | Rank requirement: | Lord/Lady |
| Power Bonus: | 20% | Cost: | 0 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 0% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 5 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | No Effect | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| The mysterious festive magic contained within the Hailstones at the Great Winter Taiga have been known to exhibit some strange, fantastical properties under certain extraordinary conditions. This base demonstrates what happens when a massive number of Hailstones are combined and compressed into one another. The result is a Hailstone singularity of infinite density and infinite holiday cheer. The overwhelming gravitational force unleashed by this base is so strong that it can bend the very fabric of spacetime in the Valour Rift, allowing MouseHunters to slingshot themselves up the tower at unprecedented speeds. However, this inescapable gravitational force has the side effect of pulling all the tower's loot past its event horizon and into the singularity where it is lost forever.<br>This base adds +1 step per catch in the Valour Rift but it consumes all loot drops while climbing the tower.<br>In addition, this base has the same properties as the Prestige Base which causes it to grow in power as the hunter reaches new heights:<br>+20 Power Boost per floor reached during Ultimate Umbra<br>+2 Luck Boost per Total Eclipse floor reached during Ultimate Umbra<br>These boosts are based on highest floor reached and are applied permanently as you reach greater heights during Ultimate Umbra. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

This base could only be obtained during Great Winter Hunt 2025 event.

After sending 50 Golems, the Hunter could claim this base from the Reward Track.

## Special Effects

- This base adds +1 step per catch in the Valour Rift but it consumes all loot drops while climbing the tower.
- Whenever the Hunter reaches their highest Ultimate Umbra Floor, the additional stats are immediately applied to their Rift Hailstone Singularity Base:
+ 20 Power Boost per Floor reached
+ 2 Luck Boost per Eclipse Floor reached

# 🛡️ Base: Royal Ruby Refractor Base

> **Type**: Bases | **Category**: Bases | **Source**: [Royal Ruby Refractor Base](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Royal_Ruby_Refractor_Base)

| Royal Ruby Refractor Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 2,000 | Rank requirement: | Viceroy |
| Power Bonus: | 20% | Cost: | 17,000,000 Gold3,000 Golden Goose Egg100 Royal Ruby Bean |
| Attraction Bonus: | 15% | Limited Edition: | No |
| Luck: | 26 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Extremely Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| By harnessing the extraordinary properties of Royal Ruby Beans and Golden Goose Eggs, the Trapsmith at the Bountiful Beanstalk was able to apply the same scientific principles that powered the Adorned Empyrean Jewel Base towards the construction of this stunning successor. The higher refractive index of Royal Ruby Beans allows for even more power to be amplified and focussed through their facets into the trap above. The impressive mouse-catching ability of this base is rivalled only by its breathtaking beauty.<br>In addition to its extraordinary mouse-catching power, this miracle of modern science has the following special effects:<br>Floating Islands: Chance to add +1 to area-specific loot drops<br>Bountiful Beanstalk: Doubles Fabled Fertilizer loot drops |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Royal Ruby Refractor Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Bountiful Beanstalk for:

- 17,000,000 Gold
- 3,000 Golden Goose Egg
- 100 Royal Ruby Bean

It requires the Hunter to own the Adorned Empyrean Refractor Base and complete the Slay the Mythical Giant King adventure to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Floating Islands
Provides a chance to increase any area-specific loot drop by 1 if dropped by the mouse on a successful catch
Area-specific loot list includes:
Adorned Empyrean Jewel
Bottled Wind
Cloud Curd
Cloudstone Bangle
Corsair's Curd
Cyclone Stone
Empyrean Seal
Empyrean Treasure Trove
Enchanted Wing
High Altitude Treasure Trove
Low Altitude Treasure Trove
Raw Ancient Jade
Sky Glass
Sky Ore
Sky Pirate Seal
Sky Sprocket
Skysoft Silk
Storm Cell
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:
My Royal Ruby Refractor Base focused the light from my Skyfarer's Oculus and discovered the following loot:
1 <item X>
1 <item Y> (additional loot discovery continues on if the chance activates for more than 1 type of loot drop)
- Bountiful Beanstalk
Doubles Fabled Fertilizer loot drops
When the effect activates, it generates a journal entry:
My Royal Ruby Refractor Base refracted and amplified the sunlight cultivating [X] additional Fabled Fertilizer!

## Related Bases

| Skello-ton Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

| Yes, it's true that zombies possess an insatiable hunger for brains. Brains this, brains that, it's brains, brains, brains all the time! But after several huge meals of brains, a zombie's tastes begin to turn to dessert, and the discerning zombie longs for nothing more than that classic treat: Skell-O!The Skello-ton Base is composed entirely of Skello, which will leave zombies' mouths watering. It's so jaw-droppingly good that the zombies will even forget to chew the brains in their mouths, letting them tumble to the ground instead. While definitely gross, this allows you to collect a portion of those Brain Bits for use in crafting Brain Charms.Even more effective when paired with the Brain Extractor or Maniacal Brain Extractor weapons! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Skello-ton Base was available for purchase for 435,000 gold, 1 Mousataur Skull and 8 Cobwebs from Haunted Terrortories Trapsmith during the Halloween 2014 event. It cannot be refunded.

## Special Effects

The Skello-ton Base collects Brain Bits from the various zombie mice. The effect is greater when paired with the Brain Extractor or Maniacal Brain Extractor weapons.

- Maniacal Brain Extractor - Increases the special effects of the Skello-ton Base.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |

| The highly unstable metallic alloy that was used to construct this base produces a bizarre chemical reaction when exposed to massive amounts of sodium chloride. When completely enveloped by salt, the outer layers of this base will begin to corrode and that process releases a noxious gas that oddly smells exactly like a Grub Scent Charm!<br>This salty base will add +1 to the salt level in the Sand Crypts upon catching a mouse with any salt charm.<br>Once the salt level reaches 50 in the Sand Crypts, this base will automatically attract the King Grub Mouse without the need for a Grub Scent Charm! |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

Smelly Sodium Base can only be obtained from opening Season 10 Treasure Chests. These chests are labeled (Season 10) in their names.

The chance of a chest containing this base increases with every chest opened.

The 30th Season 10 chest is guaranteed to contain the Smelly Sodium Base if it is not yet owned by the Hunter.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Sand Crypts
Increases the salt level by 2 upon catching a mouse with Grub Salt Charm armed
Increases the salt level by 3 upon catching a mouse with Super Salt Charm armed
Adds King Grub and removes all other mice from the attraction pool when salt level is at 50
This does not take effect if Shattering Charm is armed (and thus King Scarab is still attracted)
Upon catching the King Grub mouse with Grub Salt Charm or Super Salt Charm armed, salt level is reset to 2 or 3 respectively instead of 0

The Ultimate Chrome Base is a base.

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 1250 | Rank requirement: | Archduke |
| Power Bonus: | 10% | Cost: | #Obtained Via |

| Luck: | 25 | Crafted: | No |

| By combining the remarkable powers of all the many Chrome bases that have been discovered, the Trapsmith at the Cerulean Skyport has finally created one Chrome base to rule them all... the Ultimate Chrome Base!<br>This incredible base infuses massive quantities of Cerulean Chrome with an enormous amount of energy harnessed from Adorned Empyrean Jewels in order to power its long list of special effects.<br>The Ultimate Chrome Base has the following special effects while using it:<br>• Anywhere: Spawns a Chrome Skull every 300 hunts<br>• Anywhere: Provides a 100% chance to roll up any armed Chrome Charm into its next, more powerful version<br>• In the Tribal Isles: Increases quantity range for drops of Seashells, Savoury Vegetables, Delicious Stones, and all Seeds<br>• In the Tribal Isles: Spawns a pepper that matches whatever seed a mouse drops<br>• In the Sandtail Desert: Consumes a Flameshard every hunt, mice drop bonus loot when caught<br>• In all Living Garden related areas: Chance to generate a random essence<br>• In all Living Garden related areas: Increases petal and herb drops<br>• In the Sand Crypts: Adds +1 to the salt level upon catching a mouse with any salt charm<br>• In the Sand Crypts: Automatically attracts the King Grub Mouse when the salt level reaches 50<br>• In the Lost/Cursed City: 50% chance to break any curse upon catching a mouse<br>• In Fort Rox (Night/Dawn Phase): Chance to find Meteorite Pieces<br>• In the Queso Canyon: Consumes 5 Solidified Amber Queso every hunt, mice drop bonus loot when caught<br>• In Zokor: Activate Stealth Mode to provide a 50% chance to not lose Stealth<br>• In Zokor: Activate Heist Mode to double Stealth lost but also double Fealty, Tech, and Scholar district loot gained<br>• In Moussu Picchu: Activate Forecasting Mode to double weather intensity gained on every catch based on cheese armed<br>• In Moussu Picchu: Activate Condensing Mode to prevent the weather intensity from increasing |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Ultimate Chrome Base can be purchased from the Trapsmith in Cerulean Skyport for:

- 5,000,000 Gold
- 200 Cerulean Chrome Ingot
- 3 Adorned Empyrean Jewel

It requires the Hunter to own:

- Chrome Base
- Chrome Tribal Vegetation Kaboom Base
- Chrome Desert Heater Base
- Chrome Essential Living Garden Base
- Chrome Electromagnetic Meteorite Base
- Chrome Queso Cannonstorm Base
- Chrome Thief Base
- Chrome Stormcaster Base

And to complete the Liberate the Cerulean Skyport from Captain Crook adventure to purchase.

It cannot be refunded.

## Location-Specific Effects

- Floating Islands
Adds +1 to area-specific loot drops
Consumes 1 Cyclone Stone per catch

- Anywhere: Spawns a Chrome Skull every 300 hunts
- Anywhere: Provides a 100% chance to roll up any armed Chrome Charm into its next, more powerful version
- Tribal Isles
Increases quantity range for drops of Seashell, Savoury Vegetables, Delicious Stones, and all Seed
Spawns a pepper that matches whatever seed a mouse drops
- Sandtail Desert
Consumes a Flameshard every hunt, mice drop bonus loot when caught
- Living Garden related areas
Chance to generate a random essence
Increases Garden Petals and Herbs drops
- Sand Crypts
Adds +1 to the salt level upon catching a mouse with any salt charm
Automatically attracts the King Grub Mouse when the salt level reaches 50
- Lost City and Cursed City
50% chance to break any curse upon catching a mouse
- Fort Rox (Night/Dawn Phase)
Chance to find Meteorite Piece
- Queso Canyon
Consumes 5 Solidified Amber Queso every hunt, mice drop bonus loot when caught
- Zokor
Activate Stealth Mode to provide a 50% chance to not lose Stealth
Activate Heist Mode to double Stealth lost but also double Fealty, Tech, and Scholar district loot gained
- Moussu Picchu
Activate Forecasting Mode to double weather intensity gained on every catch based on cheese armed
Activate Condensing Mode to prevent the weather intensity from increasing

# 🛡️ Base: Vegetation Base

> **Type**: Bases | **Category**: Bases | **Source**: [Vegetation Base](https://mhwiki.hitgrab.com/wiki/index.php/Vegetation_Base)

| Vegetation Base |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Base Statistics | Base Info |  |  |
| Power: | 250 | Rank requirement: | Legendary |
| Power Bonus: | 20% | Cost: | 0 |
| Attraction Bonus: | 10% | Limited Edition: | Yes |
| Luck: | 5 | Crafted: | No |
| Cheese Effect: | Fresh | Smashable: | No |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | External Link |
| The fertile, arable soil in this base has a magical effect when seeds land upon its surface. While hunting in the Tribal Isles, any pepper seed that drops from a mouse onto this base will instantly cause a pepper to bloom without destroying the seed!<br>Use this base in the Elub Shore, Nerg Plains, or Derr Dunes to find a pepper that matches whatever seed a mouse drops. |  |  |  |

## Obtained Via

The Vegetation Base is a Limited Edition base not sold by any Trapsmith. It may only be obtained from opening a Season 7 Treasure Chest.

Each Season 7 Relic Hunter Treasure Chest has a chance of containing the Vegetation Base. The more Season 7 chests you open, the greater your chance of finding the Limited Edition base will be!
There are two Limited Edition bases in Season 7, After opening 29 chests, you're guaranteed to either 
1) find both bases in your 30th (if it is a rare chest) if you have not already found them
2) find the first base in your 30th and the second base in your 31st (if it is a normal chest) if you have not already found them.

## Specific Effects

While this base is equipped in Elub Shore, Nerg Plains, Derr Dunes, or while intercepting a Tribal Isles Raid in the Cerulean Skyport, any pepper seed that drops from a mouse onto this base will instantly cause a pepper to bloom without destroying the seed, where the number of peppers that bloom correspond to the number of pepper seeds dropped.

## Related Bases

- Chrome Tribal Vegetation Kaboom Base - Chrome version
